## Revision log: draft 8.4 (the v3.5 final version, 2026-09-28)

**What it answers.** The showrunner agreed the final version: "i think we're now seeming fully on the same page. i now trust your judgement to put everything we've discussed into the final rendering. go for it". The spec is [proposal-v35](production/full-v3/proposal-v35.md), scene by scene, with the lead's choices in [PLAN §8](production/full-v3/PLAN.md) (1A–12A). Behind it: SHOWRUNNER-NOTES 00000 (human, fast, a show: the scramble after the blow, more Act One, less after the firing, faster back-and-forth, the 2018 night, the president's deepfake back and the Senate's clone cut, Alyi's reversal made clear, the tour and Nesnej made clear) and 0000 (motives, flashbacks and milestones). The change table by beat id, the runtime, the inner-voice list, the seams, the tempo and the verification are in [script-v35-notes](production/full-v3/script-v35-notes.md).

- **Scene numbers.** The draft keeps its own scene numbers, so older cross-references hold. Each heading carries proposal-v35's number (`P35 sc N`), which the beat plans use. New scenes take letters: 7A, 8A, 8B, 10A, 10B, 11A (Act One), 15A (Act Two), 26B, 26C and 27A (Act Four).
- **Act One grows to about 7:30** ("if anything we need more in act 1"). Every rival answers him, and we see why they started:
  - **the first weeks** (sc 7A, a montage): the essay, the bug fix, the sandwich in the VCR, Nole's "scary good" reply to Mas, dinner, an apology, the banner nobody reads over a wrong sum, the coders' site banning its answers; then his own phone lights red (Elgoog's code red);
  - **3 AM** (sc 8A): a stranger's post says it helped; he reads it twice. "they've stopped testing it. they're using it." (choice 1A);
  - **JUN 2018, the night the machine taught itself** (sc 8B, memory 1 of 3): ATOD's bots playing themselves, 180 years a day; Alyi's awe, Mas's practicality ("then a lot more computers."), "What else would you build?", and a side project finishing a sentence badly in the corner;
  - Tasya wants his model "in everything we make" (sc 9); Gerg's earlier GNIB line is back (choice 7A);
  - **the window** (sc 10A): the users line off the top of the glass, "Still a preview?" / "still a preview.", all four laugh; **the vision post** (sc 10B): "Planning for AGI and beyond", three passages in his words, "someone gets to be in the room." (choice 2A), and Atem answers the same day;
  - GTP-4's bar-exam card and Alyi's awe in the glass; Mario's "Point two: if someone's going to build it, it should be someone who's scared of it." (choice 3A); CLOD's pane may become a claymation insert (timing identical either way);
  - Elgoog's waitlist (sc 11A); Nole's FILED stamp and "Now put the stamp down." (choice 4A);
  - two INVIDIA plants (the GPU the tear lands on; the 2018 racks) and **the second style leap** (choice 9A): the tear on the heatsink as a 2.5 s near-photoreal macro, objects only.
- **Act Two is one caused line** (about 3:31): the reminder and the sheet; **the Senate without the clone** (the one fake is now the president's; "i get paid enough for health insurance." is Mas's own line again); **MAR 2019, the company with a ceiling** (sc 15A, memory 2 of 3): billions, capped at 100x, "the board.", "nothing.", "Good answer.", the landlord's first billion; **taking it to the world** (sc 16, rebuilt): the passport's stamps, "if we can comply, we will, and if we can't, we'll cease operating.", NOTERB's "blackmail" post, his "no plans to leave"; the signatures scroll; every signer's pen becomes a purchase order.
- **Act Three** (about 2:19): **the president's deepfake is restored** (sc 21: the copy, "When the hell did I say that?", "which one's real?", `verified: human`), and the DevDay thought waits until the counter has been read.
- **Act Four is less after the firing** (about 8:02, 25.5 s under v3.4) and more of him:
  - the goodbye post typed twice; **the war room** (sc 26B): Gerg's "They took my chair. Told me after. So I quit.", Tasya's minute, the money's calls, "the budget. i said the budget." and the rattled count "gerg. tasya. the money. the money. the money."; **the flight home** (sc 26C) with `TERMS · 1. GERG`; the carve and **TPOOL in two shots, 6 s** (choice 5A, memory 3 of 3);
  - the board's side trimmed by choice 12A amended (the list is in the notes), keeping every beat on the version ledger's list; the desk's two props; Alyi's held face; the staff's beat of silence; Neleh's look at the blank line moved after Tasya's statement;
  - **Alyi, alone** (sc 27A, wordless): the bullpen full of boxes, the hearts on Rima's post, his hand near the users line; the same hearts carry to Mas's phone;
  - the 2 AM count and "gerg walked out for me." are cut; the board leaves the call in about 9 s; Alyi's regret is shown in full; Terb reads the agreement without his lead-in; the coda's holds are shorter.
- **The inner voice: 15 → 17 lines, 135 words.** New: the 3 AM read, the vision post's want, and the war room's two. Cut: the 2 AM count and "gerg walked out for me.". Silent from the call's first tile to "super.", at the testimony, at Neleh's paper, in every memory, on the board's side and in Alyi's night.
- **Tempo.** Quick exchanges now answer at 0.15–0.35 s (Mas at 0.4–0.5 s, and his own thought-then-line gaps unchanged); the air around scenes doesn't shrink. It's set per exchange in the plans' `tempo` fields.
- **Verified before lock** (notes §7): the tour's stops and order (Paris came before London, and Toronto before the hearing, so the stamps run Rio → Munich and the guest book names no city), the London line (TIME's wording, not the proposal's hybrid), Brussels' and his posts (read raw), the statement's signers (the count shown as "hundreds"), Nole's post (a reply to Mas), Alyi's regret, the at-capacity page (December 2022, undated), the launch banner (unverifiable, so an invented banner grounded in the launch post), INVIDIA's trillion, and Macrosoft's two posts.
- **Voices:** 33 new Kokoro takes plus one cut and one reuse (audio/ep01/v35/), each in the character's existing voice; MARIO's "Point two…" is final in his Kokoro voice (choice 11A: one film, the ElevenLabs cast with Kokoro Mario and Sirrah recast by the ElevenLabs pass). Never a clone of anyone.
- **Runtime:** story ≈ 22:22.5, episode ≈ 23:04.6 (proposal-v35 estimated 22:05 and 22:47; the notes' §2 says where the difference comes from).

## Revision log: draft 8.3 (the v3.4 round, 2026-09-28)

**What it answers.** SHOWRUNNER-NOTES 000 and [PLAN §7](production/full-v3/PLAN.md): "mas's dialogue is too much just predicting what someone is going to say next, rather than useful narration/insight into what he's thinking/planning"; "the mastermind who has higher foresight and planning than others usually", with the firing the one thing he didn't see coming; then "make sure in general writing is well nuanced within the context. don't make anything too on the nose". Also: keep one deepfake, bring back the Mario line, and (later) cut the duck. The change table is [script-v34-notes §2](production/full-v3/script-v34-notes.md).

- **The inner voice: 13 → 15 lines, 82 → 132 words, most of them his plan, hinted, not declared.**
  - Each line is a small, practical thought that reads as ordinary the first time and as foresight on a rewatch. No "plan" words and no thesis.
  - **The lines:**
    - "she's right. it will break. it goes out tonight anyway." (the launch)
    - "mostly the bill. we can't buy that many servers. someone can." (then he calls the landlord)
    - "mine's half written." (when the president wants promises in writing; the Senate pays it)
    - "my other company. for when it gets harder to tell." (the Orb)
    - "a year ago, forty users and a nice thread." (DevDay)
    - "gerg's not on it. probably the budget. good. i'll ask for more compute." (he plans right into the call)
    - "gerg walked out for me." (as he calls him)
    - **on his return:** "they had four votes. i had the landlord. the money. gerg."
  - "mario used to sit where gerg sits. he left to build a careful one." is back.
  - **Cut:** the predictions ("she'll go for three.", "gerg. he'll say he's compiling."), "he's not wrong." and "i made it for everyone else."
  - Silence from the call's first tile to "super." is unchanged. Nothing at the testimony, nothing about the firing's reasons, and nothing about the staff letter.
- **One deepfake (B):** the Senate's cloned voice stays. The May 12 altered anchor clip, its repost and hailstone (sc 14), the deepfake Nedib, "When the hell did I say that?" and "which one's real?" (sc 21) are cut. The order's one line stays, the rules written down.
  - **Balance (guardrails §2a rule 3):** with both halves of the political pair gone, Sirrah's invented catchphrase is cut too (§3.2 of the notes).
- **The duck is cut (C):** the Elgoog demo film, its Runway insert and "What the quack!" go. The tag goes from the room to the cover, the lawsuit and "noted.", and the rail DEC 6 sits on the cover.
- **The bible:** mas-inner-voice §3, §4 and §7 and calibration §5 and §9 now describe the planner voice. The overview's new §6a covers the later episodes' scheming principals.
- **Runtime:** story ≈ 20:13.5 (−15.8 s against the v3.3 lock).

**What it answers.** [PLAN §6](production/full-v3/PLAN.md), the binding work list from the v3.2 film's audit, mood analysis and newcomer read, applied as a polish: each fix sits in one spot, and the v3.2 spine ([agency-v32](production/full-v3/agency-v32.md)) is unchanged. The change table with beat ids is [script-v33-notes §2](production/full-v3/script-v33-notes.md); the plans are [beat-plan-v33/](production/full-v3/beat-plan-v33/), deltas on the v3.2 lock.

- **The voice: 11 → 13 lines** (V1, V2, both v3 takes, no new line): "it does." when the collar arrives on the landlord's hand, and "he's not wrong." before "how's the dancing?", which ends Act Two's silence. No voice at Neleh's paper (V3): his held face instead.
- **Story, one spot each:** the Tidder post answers a thread on his screen, and the VP clip and the pinky promise go (S1); Rima's replacement is shown in picture, with no line, because no clean reported reason exists (S2, see the notes); Ttemme's "Okay." goes, and the page's back is blank; "Down here." goes (S3); "Everyone's packed…" is the employee's, in her own voice (S4); "…below them, above them, around them." is a clip on the bullpen TV (S5).
- **Picture, one spot each:** Mas's face not answering the board question (P1); the million post as a preview (P2); the tear's glint (P3); the collar arrives at "That collar suits you." (P4); the Atem monitor in the room's background (P5); the chip order unmistakably in Mario's hand (P7); Act Two ends on the chip-maker's line leaving the frame, and the glass is cut (P8); his held face at page 30 and the paper's tab kept (P10); the reminder over his NOTIFY ME page (P10); the collapsed post (P11); the boardroom push-in (P12); one date at the walk-in (P13); two-part plates (P14); the count on screen (P16); the cover held (P19).
- **Judged against PLAN §6:** P6 (the altered clip redrawn as the senator) is declined, because the record's clip was an altered anchor clip (facts #18), and a repost of a fake of the senator would invent an act by real people; the anchor is drawn unmistakably as an anchor instead. S2's reason isn't written, because the only reported one is [UNVERIFIED] (facts §E).

## Revision log: draft 8.1 (the `v32-agency` pass, 2026-09-28, second round)

**What it answers.** Two inputs, from the lead: [calibration](../../bible/calibration.md), the nuance ledger (binding), and [audit-v31](production/full-v3/audit-v31.md), the audit of the v3.1 film. Everything draft 8 did stays; the full list, with each fix's code, is in [script-v32-notes §10](production/full-v3/script-v32-notes.md).

1. **The inner voice: 12 → 11 lines, 77 words** (calibration §5: each line does what only the voice can). **Cut:** "eleven keys. he's here for the twelfth." (the count a newcomer can't read; the key ring is shown large instead), "neleh's on our board. she quoted us." (a caption; her plate carries it), "those are stills." (a caption; the stutter carries the joke). **Restored:** "i made it for everyone else." and "it looks calmer than me.", the two caught lines only the voice can do (the audit keeps both).
2. **Plates** (calibration §3): each principal's first plate gets one relation word: `GERG MOCKBRAN · CO-FOUNDER`, `RIMA TAMURI · CTO`, `ALYI · CO-FOUNDER`, `TASYA · MACROSOFT` (his phone contact; the lobby card stays `TASYA / THE LANDLORD`), `RADNUS · RUNS ELGOOG`, `MARIO · EX-NOPEAI`, `NOLE · EARLY FUNDER`, `LAHTNEMULB · CHAIRMAN`, `KRAM · RUNS ATEM`, `NELEH · NOPEAI BOARD`, `ADELINA · CO-FOUNDER`, and Rima's tile `INTERIM CEO`. **No `(REPORTED)` label** is needed anywhere.
3. **Cause, then effect, on Sunday** (the audit's #3, calibration's chain test): his walk into the lobby as a guest moves *inside* the board's side, before their Sunday, and steps out into their camera; the board's "we're no closer" now follows from his move, and the time away from him splits in two. His side opens at 2 AM on the count again.
4. **"As you know" lines rewritten as wants** (the audit's #5): Gerg's "Elgoog's going to hear about this from a search box."; Oigneb's "You signed it. Now put the iron down."; Neleh's "Step three. Rima, the staff will come to you now." (Alyi's echo goes; the tile says INTERIM CEO); Sunday's "The staff want him back…" cut.
5. **Recap and procedure cut** (calibration §2: talk away from Mas has to change his situation): Neleh's read-aloud of the blog post goes (the post types itself; Terb keeps the device); Tasya's turn-limit speech becomes "House rules, Sydney." with the timer's face; the Act Three LEDs omen goes.
6. **Moves, sharpened** (calibration §9: full sentences when he asks or decides): the call is "it's the bill. we're going to need more servers."; the Tidder post follows straight from the forum's raised hands; he posts the tour's reversal with his own thumb; he closes Neleh's paper and opens the order.
7. **Four lines lead a new place** (the audit's #11): Nole's "Great sign.", "Is this a coup?", Tasya's "Don't get up, Mas." and the memo's first words, each marked as a J-cut.
8. **Picture notes** for the shot passes: the act-out glass (a face on the water, no body), the flame with Radnus's face, the key ring large, the collar's clink, Mada's face on the reminder, the heart count on screen, the two tally marks legible, the lit window at one shot, Mario's finger on "trained".
9. **Runtime:** story **≈ 20:42.4** (−27.2 s against the v3.1 lock).

## Revision log: draft 8 (the `v32-agency` pass, 2026-09-28)

**What it answers.** [SHOWRUNNER-NOTES](../../production/SHOWRUNNER-NOTES.md) notes 00 and 0: "it felt like a bunch of altman inner thoughts felt forced… it is not really showing him take any action, just existing through the moments. if he is the main character he should be showing agency", and then "mas's lack of agency is very apparent… makes it feel like watching a random sequence of events [rather] than about mr mas's rise to power", with "make sure no previous advice was taken too extreme". The v3 newcomer said the same: "I couldn't tell what Mas wants: he is carried along, and others win Act Four for him."

**1. The spine: his rise to power, one step per act, each caused by his last move** (the full map, scene by scene, is [agency-v32.md](production/full-v3/agency-v32.md)).
- **Act One, he launches and secures the money.** The launch is his call (Gerg builds it and leaves him the button). He announces the million himself. The bill is his problem, so **he calls the landlord**: "it's the bill." / "I'll bring a pen." The check that jams the lobby door is the answer, and the pen clipped to it is the pen he writes `PLEASE` with. GTP-4 goes out on his click, the same button.
- **Act Two, he makes himself the face of it.** He takes the seat nearest the teacher and owns the class photo. At the Senate **he proposes the agency himself**, in the record's words, and slides `PLEASE REGULATE ME` across as he says it; they ask him to run it *because* he proposed it, and he declines ("i love my current job."). He stamps his own tour's added dates.
- **Act Three, he consolidates.** He switches the monitor off and **walks onto the DevDay stage live**: "and today, you can build your own chatgtp." at 100 million a week. A week later there are more people than servers, and **he pauses the sign-ups himself** (his real post). The board's invite arrives next; nothing links the two.
- **Act Four, the fall and the climb back.** After the blow **he posts his own words** ("…will have more to say about what's next later."). His side opens on **the lobby he walks into as a guest**, told twice: their camera saw a small figure; now he puts the badge on and posts it. At 2 AM he calls Gerg and asks for the letter, says "keep building.", and at the landlord's door **picks up the MACROSOFT badge and doesn't wear it**. On Tuesday he accepts the invite, makes the new chair's extinguisher work, gives up the seat and names his term. The return is won by what he built (the people, the landlord's desks, the money) and by those moves.

**2. What he never does** (guardrails §1-2 and §6). No invented motive at a contested moment: every move is his public record (a post, testimony, a keynote) or a small invented stake (the call, the pen, the seat, the stamp, the badge, the pin). He never orchestrates the staff revolt: the letter is the staff's, he asks to hear it after it exists. No speech or voice-over announces ambition; he isn't a declared schemer. His agency shows as precise moves with consequences, which is how he wins without being seen to play.

**3. The inner voice: 28 lines → 12, 208 words → 83.** No new line; every kept line has its take. What stays: the gap ("she's right. it will break.", "i know. i still read it twice.", "mostly the bill."), three reads that pay ("she'll go for three.", "eleven keys. he's here for the twelfth.", "gerg. he'll say he's compiling."), the board seed ("neleh's on our board. she quoted us."), the one effort line ("thrilled is too much."), the misread before the blow, the silence at the blow, "i don't keep score.", the lost count at 2 AM, and "those are stills." Sixteen go: every thought that could be an action became one (the million, the seat, not turning, the hand coming down, the call), and every line that glossed the picture or explained a person goes. Act Two has no inner voice at all now; he performs in public there. The map is in the notes (§2).

**4. Kept.** The shock opening, told twice; the board's side whole; every restoration (Sydney, the Atem thread, the hands runner, Rezeile's op-ed, the Runway demo); the conversations; every v3.1 fix. Where a cut V.O. line was a v3.1 fix (the bay's read, the Orb's origin, the Radnus rehearsal), its job moves into the picture (the tag held to read and his replay, the shipping label, Radnus's moving lips); the notes (§3.4) list each.

**5. Runtime.** Story **≈ 21:09.7**, level with the v3.1 lock's 21:09.6: the new moves (≈ +40.8 s: five new beats and five that grow) are paid for by the voice-over they replace and the air it held (≈ −40.8 s across nineteen beats). Ranked trims to about 21:00 are in the notes (§1.2).

**Not in this pass:** takes, timelines, art, the score, the calibration guide ([calibration](../../bible/calibration.md), being written, governs where it disagrees).

## Revision log: draft 7 (the `v31-script` pass, 2026-09-27)

**What it answers.** The showrunner's notes on the v3 film ([PLAN §5](production/full-v3/PLAN.md#5-v31-the-finalizing-round-from-2026-09-27-1600)): "a few confusing transitions that just seem to come out of nowhere"; "bring back the syney and atem references"; the beginning of Act Four "should feel like a sudden shock... the video call is a bit hard to understand what cancel means"; and "i'll let you make your own review, judgements, and update to the full next version". It folds in the two reviews ([newcomer](production/full-v3/read-v3-newcomer.md), [critic](production/full-v3/review-v3-critic.md)) and the story fixes of the [mood analysis](production/full-v3/mood-analysis.md). Every change, with the finding it answers, is in [script-v31-notes §3](production/full-v3/script-v31-notes.md).

**1. Act Four opens on the shock (the lead's design).**
- **His side:** the suite's ordinary life (the crane, the glasses, the Orb following a practice lap: about 13 s), then the JOIN with his one wrong read ("gerg's not on it. alyi set it up. probably just the budget."). Alyi's first sentence reaches us over a thinned LEVERAGE, then a bright hard cut to a literal host dialog in the 1993 look, `Remove MAS MANALT from the meeting?` `[Remove]`, ALYI's pointer clicks it, and the one silence, the buzz, "super.". "the race is tomorrow…" is cut.
- **The board's side** opens on Neleh's desk at 11:52: Mada has joined early, and "Once more, before the others join." turns THE PLAN into her own prep. Then 11:59, Alyi's lines whole, and the same Remove from their side (`ALYI removed MAS MANALT from the meeting.`). Her gag card moves here. The lobby's return gag greys **Remove**. The TPOOL flash goes.
- **The board's side is trimmed** by about 18 s inside, so with THE PLAN it runs 3:37 (it would have been 3:49).

**2. Mas's want, and his moves.** His want stays legible: to be noticed and liked ("let's see if anyone notices.", "it likes me."), managed ("thrilled is too much."), credited ("i've had mine up since may."). In Act Four he makes the moves:
- he **calls** Gerg and **asks** him, "read me the letter.";
- to "Do I tell everyone to pack?" he answers "keep building.";
- at Tasya's door he asks the price first ("and the rent?", Act One's words, before he has stepped on anything) and keeps the door open;
- on Tuesday he **accepts** the board's invite looking at it, **makes** Terb's extinguisher work (the pin, set up with a dry click), gives up the seat ("we'll stand.") and names his one term, "gerg comes back too.".
No invented motive sits at a contested moment: the moves are small, invented actions and his own words, and he is silent inside at the door and the terms.

**3. The board is seeded.** At launch night Rima asks the room "Did anyone tell the rest of the board?" and Gerg answers "It's a research preview."; nobody else answers, and Mas clicks. In Act Three Neleh's real paper is a beat he reads ("neleh's on our board. she quoted us."), and the reminder in the act-out names the four attendees on hover.

**4. The reversal is shown.** After Mario says no, the Sunday boardroom has the phones in a row (`STAFF · STAFF · INVESTORS · INVESTORS`), a ticker (`INVESTORS PUSH TO BRING MANALT BACK`), and Neleh: "The staff want him back. The investors want him back." Then "We've talked all day about him coming back, and we're no closer." The Monday letter lands on the board in the avalanche (its header rides the first tile).

**5. Transitions.** The bay gets a match cut (the print in his hand becomes his phone, whose feed scrolls from the class photo to the altered clip) and one read: "her mouth is a beat late. the voice isn't hers." The chairman then says the record's words, "That voice was not mine. The words were not mine." The lighthouse item goes; Tuesday gets its invite; DevDay opens on the two-shot; the duel loses its tape gag; Gerg's Tidder call is a video tile; the Orb's box gets one orienting line; the pause letter's act-out has a stake (EMIT on his desk, Alyi reading it) and reads `PLEASE` / `REG`.

**6. Restored, each as Mas's problem.**
- **Sydney** (sc 10): ChatGTP's face in GNIB's colours, the record's four sentences, his compliment-knife, the landlord's egg timer, the reset, Gerg's closed laptop.
- **The Atem leak:** the thread on Gerg's laptop and "give it a minute. it'll be open source."; paid at Act Three's arrival by the landlord's thirteenth key and "thirteen.".
- **The hands runner** (about 13 s): one held room frame; Sirrah's two letters, the pinky promise, Remuhcs's forum, Nole's referee; framed by "i've had mine up since may.".
- **Rezeile's op-ed:** it lands on his desk with a thud, on top of the pause letter.

**7. Lines.** The critic's U1–U4, U6 and U8 are applied, U7 by the restaging, and U3 by the Orb's toast. **"gerg never waits to be asked." is kept** and moved onto Gerg's question, where the man who never waits is waiting for once; "he's typing like it's launch night." is cut instead. "Is that a tear?", the shorter lease, "Everybody was about to get paid." and "Step four, Mada?" answer the newcomer's stumbles.

**8. The band prompt is gone.** The lit-UI prompt in the bottom letterbox ("Push button", "Push research preview", the tag's "Look at glass of water") never appears; the cursor and the button live in the scene. Rails and the V.O. line are unchanged.

**9. The tag's Runway insert.** ELGOOG's demo film plays native on his monitor and stutters into stills (the demo's own "What the quack!", no caption), with one read: "those are stills."

**10. Runtime.** Story **≈ 20:58** (v3 lock 20:39.6 with the new cold open; +18.5 s). The additions (≈ +109 s) are paid for by whole beats cut (≈ −31 s: the lighthouse, the duel's last two phrases, the Senate's back-stamp, the TPOOL flash, the Other Yrral's nod, the shut door, his eyes at noon, the bay's first push) and trims inside kept beats (≈ −59 s: the lease speech, the board's side, "Chat, we're so back.", the second deepfake, Rima's forty emails, and air inside scenes). No arrival and no hold at a scene's edge was trimmed, apart from the coda's one beat the mood analysis asked for.

**Inner voice:** 28 lines, 208 words (v3: 24 and 185). Six are new, one is changed, two are cut, two moved. The map is in the notes (§2).

**Not in this pass:** takes, timelines, art, the score, facts rows beyond the notes' list, the guardrails rulings.

## Revision log: draft 6 (the `v3-script` pass, 2026-09-27)

**What it answers.** The showrunner's four notes of 2026-09-27 ([SHOWRUNNER-NOTES](../../production/SHOWRUNNER-NOTES.md) 1–4):
- let us into Mas's head, with nothing forced or corny;
- arrive in scenes and let them land;
- cut about three minutes of beats nobody cares about;
- vary the music;
- no disclaimers and few pointers: "the goal is a story, not a documentary".

The plan is [production/stick/v3-plan.md](production/stick/v3-plan.md), the voice guide is [mas-inner-voice](../../bible/mas-inner-voice.md), and the scene rules are [flow-and-continuity §2a](../../bible/flow-and-continuity.md#2a-arriving-and-leaving-scenes). The showrunner's authorization for this attempt: "i thought the previous work was close enough that i want you to just do a full episode attempt with your best judgement."

**1. Mas's inner voice: 3 lines → 24 lines, 185 words.** The lines sit in seven rooms he is reading: launch night, the lobby, the White House, the dark room, Vegas at noon, 2 AM, and the tag.
- **The sample's eleven lines are kept as recorded.** One exception: "alyi set it up. probably just the budget." gains its first sentence, "gerg's not on it.", and needs a new take.
- **Three predictions pay off** ("she'll go for three.", "he's here for the twelfth.", "gerg. he'll say he's compiling."). **One read is wrong** (the budget).
- **Four lines are caught by the picture**, under the guide's quarter.
- **He is silent:**
  - from the JOIN click through "super.";
  - on the whole board's side;
  - at every contested or credibility moment: the Tidder post and its edit, the Senate, the tour, Alyi's regret, "leave it open.", the terms.
- **The word count is under the guide's 200–300.** The sample set a terse register, and the §8 corny test rejected every padding candidate tried. The notes (§2) list the rejects, and the candidate lines if the lead wants more.

**2. The cut list (v3-plan §3), C1–C17: all applied, six with a sliver or a fix.**

---

**Notation.**
- **Scene headings** carry a number, the place and time, the style tag, the mode letter (I in the moment · S spectacle · M macro · F flashback · P THE PLAN), and a `MUSIC —` line giving the sequence's mood.
- **Beat ids** in brackets (`[5.02]`) sit on every shot and point at the beat plans. Each shot also gives its planned length and its craft markers:
  - `ARRIVE n s`: room before the first line;
  - `HOLD n s`: after the last line;
  - `J-CUT`: the next sound or line starts under the outgoing shot;
  - `L-CUT`: sound carries over the next picture.
- **Every line in quotation marks is real** and carries its tag and date: [P] primary · [P✓] re-fetched · [V] verified · [H] headline words only (chyrons and cards, never voiced) · [K†] re-verify before lock · [V/K] facts file and timeline disagree, treated as [K†]. Parody names are swapped in silently; the originals are in [facts.md](facts.md). Trimmed quotes carry ellipses, which are print marks and never a trail in the read.
- **Everything else is invented** and marked `[INVENTED]`.
- **Subtitles (draft 7):** the marks above are notation only. On screen, a spoken real line is subtitled like any other line, without quotation marks or print ellipses (the newcomer read the marks as a speech being quoted). Posts, pages, chyrons and cards keep their own typography.
- **No band prompt (draft 7):** the lit-UI prompt in the bottom letterbox ("Push button", "Look at glass of water") never appears; it read as a burned-in bug. The rails and the V.O. line keep their places, and a cursor, where there is one, lives in the scene.
- **Mas's speech is lowercase.** Posts appear as pop-ups in their own UI, never as speeches.
- **`MAS (V.O.)` is his inner voice:**
  - present tense, lowercase, never "you", never the Orb's;
  - one line in his cyan above the band, typed at his rate;
  - each line carries its `v3-vo-NN` id and its kind (read · prediction · warmth · gap · count · effort · caught).
- **Anyone else heard off picture** is `(O.S.)` or `(filter)`.
- **`RAIL:`** is the dimmed UI band that carries dates and places. There are no side badges and no truth labels.
- **`PLATE:`** is a name under a character's first appearance. **`CARD:`** is a 2-TONE name card: the world freezes for a beat. **`CARD (FULL FREEZE):`** holds the freeze while Mas moves through it (Tasya and Terb only).
- **Shot tags:**
  - `[W]` wide;
  - `[M]` medium;
  - `[2S]` two-shot;
  - `[OTS]` over-the-shoulder;
  - `[MCU]` frameless close-up; `[MCU·glass]` framed in a reflection; `[MCU·PF]` with the fallaway;
  - `[CU]` full-frame close-up;
  - `[ECU]` insert;
  - `[HIGH]` / `[LOW]` angles;
  - `[POV]` his screen, full-bleed; `[SCR]` a screen with its bezel;
  - `[SPLIT]` the meanwhile split;
  - `[GFX]` a full-frame graphic.
- **Framing rules:**
  - Mas is always at frame left.
  - A box appears only where the world has one.
  - Every close shot is its own drawing.
- **Deliveries into the dark room** come through the server rack's slot or the monitor, never a door (X1).
- **Voices** are stock synthetic presets until the part is cast. None is chosen or tuned to sound like the real person. Nobody is cloned.

## COLD OPEN

### 1. INT. APEC CEO SUMMIT, SAN FRANCISCO — MAIN STAGE — DAY [BASE] · I · P35 sc 1

MUSIC — *poised, curious.* No score under the hall. One dry piano F4 on the freeze (MM-14 "Freeze F4"), then MM-06's beeper tier for the rewind.

`SCENE:` The host wants one last warm answer from Mas and gets the calm one he always gives. The turn is what he doesn't see: something from a lit window across the bay lands in his glass while he talks. No inner voice in the cold open: his line here is spoken, on camera.

`PLAN:`
- **The chairs:** Mas's armchair at frame left, turned three-quarters toward the host's chair at frame right. The host is only ever a hand holding a question card.
- **The table:** a shared side table between them, with two water glasses, Mas's phone face-up and dark, and his tent card.
- **The window,** upstage behind Mas: across the street, a banquet hall's gold and its standing ovation (tiny, unidentifiable silhouettes); far off across the bay, the only dark building on the skyline, with one lit window and a phone glowing in it (no label, no face).
- **The camera** stays downstage of the chairs.

SOUND: the hall (HVAC, a few hundred people being polite, a lapel mic's room). Across the street the banquet's applause runs louder than anything in here. **J-CUT:** the hall is heard for half a second under black before the series' first frame.

`RAIL: NOV 16, 2023 · SAN FRANCISCO`

`[W]` [1.01] (≈ 4.75 s · ARRIVE 1.0 s) The series' first frame, drifting slowly upstage toward the window so the eye finds all three planes: the stage, the banquet, the lit window. MAS MANALT (grey hoodie, collars peeking, THE ORB at his right shoulder) in the armchair; at frame right, the host's hand on the chair arm with its card. Mas's water line is one flat row of pixels.

**PANEL HOST** *(through the hall's PA, the card lifting a little; warm)*\
Last one, Mas. What's the best part of the job? `[INVENTED · the host is a hand, unnamed and never gendered; the question gives the real answer its trigger]`

`[MCU]` [1.02] (≈ 5.6 s; held through the answer) MAS, answering, pupils one pixel toward the host. Behind him, soft: the gold, the bay, the one lit window. As he starts, a single white dot, a HAILSTONE, leaves the lit window and arcs toward us over the water in whole-pixel steps, and drops out of the top of frame toward the skylight. We see it coming; he doesn't.

**MAS**\
*(unhurried; a lunch order; one sentence across both cuts)*\
"…i've gotten to be in the room when we sort of push the veil of ignorance back—and the frontier of discovery forward…" [V · NOV 16, 2023 · facts #45 · re-fetch before lock, or fall back to the fragment from "push"]

`[ECU]` [1.03] (≈ 1.5 s, cut in on the dash; the table from above) *Plink.* Into Mas's glass, where the hailstone bobs, its surface glyph noise with no letters. The host's glass sloshes over its rim. Mas's water line doesn't move. His phone lies face-up and dark. The tent card reads, for the whole insert: `MAS MANALT · CEO, NOPEAI`.

`[MCU]` [1.04] (≈ 2.7 s) Back on his face for "…discovery forward…". Far behind him, the lit window's phone clicks off; the banquet's applause swells into the freeze and is cut by it, mid-rise.

### 2. SAME — CONTINUOUS [2-TONE FREEZE] · I · P35 sc 2

`SCENE:` His board wants him on a call on Friday at noon. He accepts without looking. This is the episode's clock, and it states no outcome.

SOUND: the hall drops to a low filtered hum with the freeze (kept in the low mids, where a small speaker still plays it), never to silence. The phone's buzz is the one close sound.

`[W]` [2.01] (1 beat) Everything freezes into navy and cream: the hand mid-gesture, the water mid-slosh, the ovation mid-rise, the hailstone mid-bob. Two things stay in colour: Mas, and the phone. MM-14's F4, left to ring.

`[ECU]` [2.02] (1 bar, held to read) The phone lights. A calendar invite slides down its screen in a generic calendar UI: `Board sync · Fri 12:00` · `Accept` `Decline`. Under the title, four small attendee circles with no names: a doorway, a glowing page, a loading spinner, a black square. noted. `[INVENTED]`

On the word his thumb taps `Accept` below frame, eyes up. The light on his jaw steps from white to the calendar's pale blue. He sips.

### 3. SAME [BASE → 1-BIT] · I/F · P35 sc 3

`SCENE:` The Orb, his witness, rewinds the room to where this started. It aims for 2022 and overshoots.

`[2S]` [3.01] (2 s) Mas and the Orb. The iris steps from the phone to Mas (three drawings). A toast pops beside it, with a year under it that starts counting back: `rewinding…` · `2023`. MM-06 enters under the F4's decay.

`[W]` [3.02] (4.5 s) The room scrubs backward in held-drawing chunks: the ovation sits, the water climbs home, the hailstone flies back through the skylight and across the bay. The year catches on `2022` for about a second, as if this is the stop; then it slips, and the years blur past (`2019` · `2015` · `2008` · `2001`…). The toast blinks: `rewinding… too far`. The frame smears sideways and collapses onto one cyan cursor on black: the intro's first frame. The hall runs backward with the scrub (a groan on the catch, a lurch when it slips), and the rewind accelerates into the cut.

> **Cut 2026-09-27 (showrunner: "the cold open to intro is not very good transition"):** sc 4, F1.1 1993, the pillarboxed `Are you sure?` dialog. It doubled the intro's own 1993, which arrives five seconds later and now pays off "too far". The rewind lands on the intro's cursor instead. See production/full-v3/shots-coldopen.md §0.

## ACT ONE · "research preview"

*The first step of his rise (draft 8): **he launches, it explodes, and he secures the money.** Draft 8.4 (v3.5) grows the act to about 7:30, the showrunner's "if anything we need more in act 1": what people do with it, why they started, the team at its height, and his vision in his own words. Every rival answers him. Seven sequences, each in a room Mas is in, on a screen he's holding, or in his memory:*
- *launch night and the drill (sc 5–7, P35 sc 4–9): the button is his call, against Rima's caution and Alyi's question. Five days later he announces the million himself, and the bill arrives with it, so he calls the landlord;*
- *the first weeks (sc 7A–8A, P35 sc 10–12, new): strangers' chats pour off his phone; the rival's alarm is among them; at 3 AM a stranger's post says it helped, and he reads it twice;*
- *the night the machine taught itself (sc 8B, P35 sc 13, new): June 2018, the first of Ep1's three memories. Why they want AGI, and who each of them is;*
- *the lobby (sc 9–10, P35 sc 14–17): the landlord's check jams the door, the answer to his call; the landlord wants his model in everything it makes; weeks on, their own chatbot comes out of the landlord's TV and scolds him;*
- *the window and the vision post (sc 10A–10B, P35 sc 18–19, new): the team at its height, then his vision, in public, and a rival answering the same day;*
- *the duel (sc 11–11A, P35 sc 20–22): a leaked rival model on Gerg's laptop, then GTP-4 goes out on his click on the day his old colleague's lab launches too; Elgoog behind a velvet rope;*
- *the pause letter (sc 12, P35 sc 23–24): the world asks the race he started to stop; Nole signs with one hand and files his own company with the other; Mas writes his own ask. The act-out.*

*Draft 8.4's scene headings keep the draft's own numbers, so every older cross-reference still holds; each heading carries proposal-v35's scene number (`P35 sc N`), which the v3.5 beat plans use.*

*What he wants is plain from the first scene and never said: to be noticed and liked ("let's see if anyone notices.", "it likes me."), the compute to keep going ("it's the bill."), and the power to build it (the button). Each want becomes a move.*

*People are introduced by what they want in their own lines, and each principal's first plate carries one relation word (draft 8.1, calibration §3): `GERG MOCKBRAN · CO-FOUNDER`, `RIMA TAMURI · CTO`, `ALYI · CO-FOUNDER`, `TASYA / THE LANDLORD`, `MARIO · EX-NOPEAI`.*

### 5. INT. NOPEAI BULLPEN — NIGHT [BASE] · I (the home room) · P35 sc 4–6

MUSIC — *warm and curious, then giddy.* For its first minute, only Gerg's Build (the chip) and Mas's felt, low under the talk: the show's own colours (mood §4 #2; the jazz-trio opening read as a lounge). The trio's warmth arrives as a payoff, when the chat flatters him (5.10), and lifts into SET-PIECE SWING on the counter's first tick. Every stop stays.

`SCENE:` Gerg has built it and wants it out tonight. Rima wants it kept small, bargains for a banner, and asks Mas what it will cost them. Alyi turns cost into dread. **The call is Mas's** (draft 8): Gerg has staged everything and left him the button, Rima says so to his face, and he answers both of them with "it's a preview." The turn is his click, knowing (we hear him think it will break). The cost is Rima's third underline, one practical question nobody answers (did anyone tell the rest of the board?), and Alyi's quiet count of the years they've shared. Then the thing he launched flatters him, and he likes it: what he wants, from the first scene, is to be noticed and liked. *(Draft 8: no voice over the wide; each of the three says what they want in their own lines.)*

`PLAN:` *(the bullpen: sc 5–7, 11, 12, 14 and Act Four's bullpen)*
- **Mas's end desk** at frame left: his laptop (dark until the launch), the beige button, his glass, and the floor tile in front of it (sc 6's hole). He sits turned three-quarters to frame right.
- **Gerg's desk** faces his across the aisle, at frame right, with Gerg's laptop glowing green.
- **The right wall:** Rima's whiteboard, and further back the conference room's glass wall. Its doorway is out of frame; Alyi's reflection lives in that glass.
- **Also in the room:** a wall monitor over the whiteboard, the hallway's tungsten spill at back left, and the window onto the bay.
- **The camera** stays on the aisle side.

SOUND: the bullpen's bed under all of sc 5–7: server hum through the floor, one buzzing tube, Gerg's keys.

`[ECU]` [5.01] (3 s · ARRIVE 1 s of room tone) A small beige button on a desk, in the exact screen position where the 1993 `OK` sat. Its 7 px label: `research preview`.

`RAIL: NOV 30, 2022` (on the wide, from its first second)

`[W]` [5.02] (≈ 6.5 s · ARRIVE: the whole shot) The bullpen after hours, held: long desks, one ceiling lamp out, tungsten from the hallway.
- GERG MOCKBRAN hunches over a laptop glowing green from below.
- RIMA TAMURI stands at the whiteboard, jacket perfect; on the board, in her hand, `LAUNCH: LOW-KEY`, underlined twice.
- In the conference-room glass hangs the reflection of ALYI, standing in a doorway that isn't in frame: a real man, seen only in the glass.

### 8B. INT. NOPEAI'S FIRST OFFICE — NIGHT — JUN 2018 [T3 · FLASHBACK] · F · P35 sc 13 *(new in draft 8.4; memory 1 of 3)*

MUSIC — *wonder and warmth:* felt piano and the Build's chip, soft (a new cue); the arena's tinny game audio plays inside it; the servers' fans. Out on the glowing line's sweep.

`SCENE:` The night the machine taught itself. On a wall of monitors, bots play bots at ATOD and get better with nobody teaching them. Alyi is in awe, because nobody taught it. Mas asks what it needs, because that's his job. Neither of them knows the road, and one question between two partners says why they want it at all. Only Mas glances at the small side project in the corner, finishing a sentence badly. *(The showrunner: "show some rl or similar innovation where mas and alyi or similar talk about making machine that can do everything is most meaningful work… a glimpse into the process that led up to chatgpt before llms when they were less sure in their exact way to agi". Placed here, after the first weeks and 3 AM, not straight after the launch; the lead's choice over the DevDay placement, PLAN §8. No inner voice in a memory.)*

*Style: the 2015–2019 memory tier (T3, cut-paper with paper grain, flashback-map §0.1), lit by the monitors. The arena on the monitors is drawn top-down in its own game medium (the milestones layer: each machine in its own medium), not in the room's style.*

`RAIL: JUN 2018`

`[W]` [v35-13.01] (≈ 3.2 s · ARRIVE: the whole shot) NopeAI's first office, night. A wall of monitors: a top-down arena on every screen, five bots against five bots, a match on each, teaching themselves. The racks behind them carry INVIDIA's logo, legible (the second plant). At the top of the wall, the counter: `PLAYED AGAINST ITSELF TODAY: 180 YEARS`. MAS, with his glass, and ALYI stand before it. GERG codes at a desk in the background, the green glow under his chin. ## ACT TWO · "the regulate-me tour"

*The second step of his rise (draft 8): **he makes himself the face of it**, to Washington and then the world. Draft 8.4 (v3.5) runs it as one caused line: the president wants promises in writing; Mas finishes his and takes it to the Senate; he won't run the agency, so he takes his rules to the world himself; the race's leaders sign one sentence about extinction; the same day, they all order more chips. Four sequences:*
- *the White House (sc 13, P35 sc 25): he's already in the seat nearest the teacher, and in the class photo only he looks at the lens;*
- *the window into the Senate (sc 14–15, P35 sc 26–27): the class photo on his phone that night, and a reminder, the Senate; the hearing opens straight on the witness table (draft 8.4: **no fake voice in this act**, the Senate's clone is cut); **he proposes the agency himself** and hands over his ask as he says it, so they ask him to run it, and he declines; he shows he owns nothing, in his own words;*
- *the company with a ceiling (sc 15A, P35 sc 28, new): March 2019, the second of Ep1's three memories. Why he owns nothing: he drew the board on top himself;*
- *the tour and the signing (sc 16–17, P35 sc 29–30A): he carries his rules to the world, threatens to leave Europe, is called a blackmailer and posts that he's staying; the race's leaders sign one sentence; the chip seller rings up every one of them.*

*His want, in this act: every room. The White House, the Senate (he's asked because he asked first), the world. One inner-voice line, at the White House: when the president wants promises in writing, his is already half written (draft 8.3); none at the hearing, in 2019 or on the tour.*

*The four CEOs in the photo were all met in Act One, so the photo plays on faces. The new faces (SIRRAH, NEDIB, SUCRAM, NESNEJ) get their gag cards.*

## ACT THREE · "verified: human"

*The third step of his rise (draft 8): **he consolidates.** The act opens in Mas's dark room, the home room the intro showed, on the device it uses: the monitor, where the landlord is welcoming someone else. A witness arrives (the Orb, his own other company). The summer arrives on the monitor: everyone in power now asks for the rules he asked for in May; he posts, and edits; a member of his own board writes about his launch; the president signs an order, and a cut-paper fake of him pops up beside him (draft 8.4: the episode's one fake). **Then he stops watching**: he switches the monitor off and walks onto the DevDay stage, live, and launches the thing that lets anyone build their own. A week later more people want in than his servers can hold, and he shuts the door himself. Friday arrives on his phone. His moves raise his power and the stakes; nothing in the episode says why the board acts, and nobody on the board watches any of it.*

*MUSIC for the act — **intimate, quiet, a little lonely.** The Water Line (MM-01, DARK ROOM), warm, as one continuous performance edited on phrase boundaries across sc 18–22. The monitor's items play their own audio inside it; it thins to one felt note under the V.O. and under every real line. THE CLOCK takes over only for the act-out. SOUND for the act: the dark room's bed (the rack's fans, its LEDs ticking in straight eighths, the cyan key's faint buzz) under every shot, and under the act break's black for 1 s before the first frame (mood §4 #9).*

`PLAN:` *(the dark room: sc 18–23, Act Four's first night and 2 AM, and the tag)*
- **Mas** at the desk at frame left, turned three-quarters toward the monitor at frame right.
- **The server rack** stands beside the desk at frame left, behind him. Its drive slot is where deliveries come in.
- **The Orb's place** is at his right shoulder, in the faded outline on the wallpaper.
- **The back wall** runs behind both him and the monitor.
- **His phone and glass** sit on the desk between him and the screen.
- **The camera** stays on the room's open side.

---

## ACT FOUR · FIVE DAYS, TOLD TWICE

*How the act is told (draft 7, with draft 8's moves).* **The fourth step (draft 8): the fall, as the consequence of his rise, and the climb back, won by what he built (the people, the landlord's desks, the money) and by his moves after the shock.**
- **Draft 8.4 (v3.5): less after the firing, more of him.** His side is surprised, and scrambles: a still face while the world spins (the showrunner: "social network is good inspiration"). The board's side is trimmed (choice 12A amended), and ends on Alyi's turn. Act Four runs about 8:02, against v3.4's 8:28.
- **Eight sequences in three chapters and a coda:**
  1. **His side, the blow, as a shock:** the suite's ordinary life, the JOIN, Alyi's sentence, the host's dialog, the drop (sc 24–26); **his first move, his own post, an hour and forty-six minutes later (draft 8), typed twice (draft 8.4)**; **the war room**, Friday afternoon into night, and **the flight home** with his terms (sc 26B–26C, draft 8.4); and his dark room that night, with TPOOL (26A). The viewer learns it the same instant he does. There is no blueprint before it.
  2. **The board's side, the explanation:** the Orb's rewind lands on Neleh's desk at 11:52, where she runs THE PLAN once more with Mada (sc 25, moved here), then noon from their side, then the boardroom on Saturday and Sunday (sc 27).
  3. **His side again, his moves:** 2 AM in the dark room (he calls Gerg; he asks for the letter; he answers Tasya and takes the badge he doesn't wear), the avalanche, Monday in the bullpen, Tuesday night in the boardroom, the lobby (sc 29–30).
  - **Inside the board's side, one scene of his (draft 8.1, the audit's #3):** Sunday afternoon he walks into his own company as a guest, puts the badge on and posts it; the picture steps out into their lobby camera, and the board's Sunday follows from his move. It also breaks the long stretch away from him in two.
  4. **The coda** (sc 31).
- **"Told twice"** means his version of noon, then the board's version of the same noon and the five days after it. On every real, tagged fact the two passes agree. Where they differ on an invented beat (his feed froze on one bar of Wi-Fi, so on his side the call ends on "company."), the board's pass is the true one. **Alyi's sentence is heard twice, verbatim,** once per side.
- **The Cancel metaphor is retired from the call.** The host's dialog says what it does, `Remove MAS MANALT from the meeting?`; its 1993 look is the only rhyme with the intro.
- **No side badges.** His voice and the camera mark the sides:
  - we hear him think once in the suite, at the JOIN (the one read he gets wrong), then nothing until "super."; in the war room, rattled, twice (draft 8.4); and once at the carve;
  - on the board's side we never hear him think: that silence is how we know we've left him;
  - his one scene inside their side, the Sunday lobby, is close on him and in colour, and it ends by stepping out into their camera (draft 8.1); at 2 AM the same hearts carry from Alyi's phone to his, and "the badge was a joke." is how we know we're back with him (draft 8.4: the 2 AM count is cut; his voice next speaks at the return).
- **Every call grid lights and names its speaking tile** (the tile's own label, the speaking ring), or the first line names its speaker.
- **The act's conversations play out.** The camera holds on them and cuts on turns.
- **Real lines are read as finished sentences;** an ellipsis is a print mark, and the subtitles drop the marks. Posts stay pop-ups. Every invented voiced line is marked `[INVENTED]`, and no invented line gives a reason for the firing. The reasoning stays in a sealed folder, as the record keeps it.
- **Sound:** every location has a room bed under every shot in it, crossfaded at changes. Music plays as one continuous performance per sequence, and thins to its pedal and ducks under talk. There are three dead stops (the Remove click's digital silence, Mada's label, "of what?").

### 27. THE FIVE DAYS, PASS ONE: THE BOARD'S SIDE [BASE] · P → I (the act's one exit) · P35 sc 44–49

MUSIC — Neleh's office clock first, then **the waltz** (BLUEPRINT, MM-07, moved here from his side) under her pointer at underscore, building as the plan draws itself; it hands to *dry, procedural comedy*, PROCEDURE in the board's colours (MM-09 a → h, one performance), on the call clock's 11:59 tick. Clockwork pizzicato between lines; under talk, the pedal and the clock-tick, ducked. Its sections change on the rooms and days, which tells a newcomer "new day, new room" without another card. Specific calls:
- On Alyi's "Mas." it drops to the pedal and stays through his tile going; under the laptop's tinny "super." the clockwork keeps going, quietly.
- The pedal only under the post.
- The crowd's hush under the all-hands.
- Mario's quartet in the split.
- One tick per grain once the hourglass turns.
- Tasya's Rhodes on the door.
- On Neleh's look at the blank line (moved after Tasya's statement, draft 8.4) the clockwork slows and hangs on one held note, then stops; Alyi's night has no score.

SOUND: Neleh's office (day, then evening); the all-hands crowd; the boardroom (HVAC, the city through glass; night on Saturday, day into night on Sunday); the lobby camera's CCTV hum.

`SCENE:` Everything plays from the board's side: her prep, their call, their desks, their boardroom, their phones. They talk to each other in full, polite, procedural sentences. The fuse is her blueprint: its steps tick as the record lands, and step 4 stays blank. Mas appears only as his public record, a small figure on a camera, a small still tile, and his voice through their laptop. **No inner voice anywhere in this pass: we've left him.** What a newcomer learns here:
- who can fire a CEO, and how (THE PLAN);
- why the board acted, as the board puts it (the post, read once, and Alyi's answer to the staff);
- that Rima is the interim CEO, and that the board told even her nothing more;
- that Gerg quit;
- that the staff and investors are calling;
- that the rival lab said no;
- that the staff and the investors want him back, and the talks went nowhere;
- why there's a second interim CEO;
- that Macrosoft has just hired Mas and Gerg.

*Anyone framed alone faces from the right. Screens keep their bezels (`[SCR]`): this is the world's view.*

*Draft 8.4 (choice 12A amended): the pass is trimmed where it repeated itself, and keeps every beat the version ledger lists: THE PLAN; "Is his feed frozen?" / "No. That is just him." and "super." through their laptop; the post; "We'll say we will."; Alyi's "Gerg has never waited to be asked."; footnote three; Mario's offer and "no"; the walk-in; "They gave him a guest badge."; "We'd like a different one." and the blank page; the hourglass; Tasya's statement; and Neleh's look at the blank line, moved to just after the statement, where step four being blank hurts most. Cut: "Three of us stepped down this year.", the removal notice, the overhead tick, "Any objections?", Neleh's "Nobody asked him to go…" (choice 6A), the eulogy card and the wall screen, "What they actually want to know…", "Then we'll write step four ourselves.", "the boardroom, now night", `F F F F`, Tasya's sign, "Step four, Mada?" and Mada's held note. New: the desk's two props, Alyi's held face after the click, the staff's one beat of silence.*

"I am deeply pleased by this result, after ~72 very intense hours of work." [V · NOV 21, 2023]

**TTEMME** *(O.S., to his stream)*\
Chat, we're so back. `[INVENTED · a5-30-18 · cut in draft 7 (R7), back in the lock since; the ledger keeps the CHAT: LIVE plate so it lands]`

The hourglass shatters, only the glass. The sand holds its shape for one beat, then falls. **J-CUT:** the lobby's neon buzz under the shatter's tail.

INT. NOPEAI LOBBY — NIGHT.

`[LOW]` [S8.01] (4 s · ARRIVE 1 s) The lobby at night, from low, the neon's buzz; then the wall sign that was blank in Act One lights up over us, on a brass stab: `DAYS SINCE SOMEONE TRIED TO FIRE MAS: 0`. Mas is already at the reception desk, wearing no badge of either kind, his glass in his hand. Under the sign, a maintenance hand sets down a small box full of spare `0` plates.

`[OTS-W]` [S8.03] (3 s) Over Mas's shoulder onto the lobby: the host's dialog from noon pops up once more, in its 1993 cream: `Remove MAS MANALT from the meeting?` · `[ Remove ]`. Remove greys out one dither step a beat. An arrow pointer steps in on the last grey step, the noon arrow's design but with its name tag empty, onto the greyed Remove, and clicks it. The button doesn't go down. *Bonk.* The dialog shakes, refused. *(Draft 7: it rhymes the dialog the viewer saw at noon; the old `OK · Cancel` pointed at a dialog nobody saw.)*

`[CU]` [S8.04] (≈ 5.8 s) MAS, full frame: the firing's drawing, with the lobby's tungsten behind him instead of the Strip's neon. At the blow this drawing was silent; now we hear him.

**MAS (V.O.)** `[v34-vo-11 · a count · draft 8.3: the return's tally, what he'd put in place (the landlord he called, the money, Gerg); never the staff, the letter or the firing's reasons]`\
they had four votes. i had the landlord. the money. gerg.

`[ECU]` [S8.05] (≈ 3.3 s · HOLD 1.5 s) His hand sets the glass down on the reception desk's pale stone and nudges it one pixel true. Over the hands:

**MAS** *(off his face, over the hands)*\
okay. `[INVENTED]`

The felt cadence settles onto the vault's F.

### 31. INT. NOPEAI BULLPEN — BACK WALL — DAY [BASE] · M/I · CODA · P35 sc 56

MUSIC — *the coda.* The vault's F hum as the pedal; no motif under the memo (the record).

`SCENE:` What the return leaves in the building: a vault nobody explains, a memo read to the staff who stayed, a nameplate unscrewed, and a chair for the landlord.

`RAIL: NOV 22, 2023`

`[ECU]` [S8.06] (≈ 2.2 s; draft 8.4: a tighter vault shot) A squat steel vault stencilled `Q*`, under the hall's tungsten. It hums at the score's root note, F. *(v3: no rail explains it. The vault and its note are the whole beat.)*

`[MCU-2]` [S8.07] (≈ 6.5 s; draft 8.4: the exchange quick, Gerg 0.25 s after "it's a preview."; the rack and his exit tighter) The frameless 50/50: MAS, left, already past the vault and not looking, the Orb at his shoulder looking at it; GERG, right, laptop open, the green under his chin; the vault soft between them, one yellow sticky note on its door: `DO NOT OPEN. DO NOT EXPLAIN.`

**GERG** *(cheerful, to Mas's back, a hand on the vault)*\
Is it ready? `[INVENTED]`

**MAS**\
*(not looking, already past; quick, not clipped)*\
it's a preview. `[INVENTED]`

**GERG** *(to his back, cheerful, literal)*\
You said that about the last one. `[INVENTED · the last one was launch night's, and everyone showed up]`

Mas keeps walking. RACK to the vault, and both men go soft. Gerg reads the sticky note, nods, and walks out of frame.

`RAIL: NOV 29, 2023`

`[W]` [S8.08] (≈ 5.8 s; a slow whole-pixel drift) **J-CUT:** the memo's first words 1.0 s over the vault (draft 8.1, the audit's #11). The bullpen by day: coats off, the boxes open and being unpacked, the staff turned toward MAS at his end desk. He reads his memo to the room, unhurried; the line crosses the cut.

**MAS** *(to the room)*\
"i love and respect alyi, i think he's a guiding light of the field and a gem of a human being. i harbor zero ill will towards him." [V · NOV 29, 2023 · facts §B logs the first and last clauses; the middle clause is mid §3: re-fetch before lock]

`[ECU]` [S8.09] (3.5 s) The boardroom: the back of a board chair, scorched by the fire, while "…i harbor zero ill will towards him." plays on. A maintenance worker's screwdriver takes the `ALYI` nameplate off it: four screws on four of his words. The plate comes off, and its clean outline stays.

*(Cut in draft 7, R12 and mood §4 #12: S8.09b, the shut door, so the film ends once, not twice.)*

`[W]` [S8.10] (≈ 3.8 s · a shorter hold; the act's last image, kept: the landlord gets a seat in the room) By the bullpen's window a MACROSOFT-blue folding chair unfolds itself. Its seat reads `MACROSOFT · OBSERVER (NON-VOTING)` [V]. It stays empty. From somewhere above, Tasya's key ring drops onto the seat. *Jangle.* HOLD on the chair. **L-CUT:** the vault's F pedal carries into the tag.

## TAG · "december"

*One thread: what the year leaves on Mas's desk. One place (the dark room), one cue, one stop (the thud), one button ("noted.").*

### 32. INT. MAS'S DARK ROOM — NIGHT [BASE] · I · P35 sc 57

MUSIC — *quiet, wry.* MM-12 december picks up the vault's F pedal and turns it into the Water Line's felt, continuous through both scenes. It thins under the cover's real line and stops once, on the THUD. SOUND: the dark room's bed, crossfaded in from the bullpen's.

`SCENE:` The year hands Mas its verdict, CEO OF THE YEAR: the top of the climb. He holds it up beside his face, and the two wear the same expression. The Orb trusts the cover a little less than the man ("that was close."). He pins the cover up and frames the badge. Then the next fight arrives by thud. *(Draft 8.3, the showrunner: the Elgoog duck demo is cut. The tag goes from the room to the cover, the lawsuit and "noted.".)*

`[2S]` [32.01] (≈ 3.2 s · ARRIVE: the whole shot) Mas and the Orb at the desk, three marks in the wood now. The monitor behind them is dark. *(v3, C12: the monitor's eggs stay cut.)*

*(Cut in draft 8.3: v31-32.01d, the Elgoog demo film, the Runway insert and "What the quack!". The monitor stays dark through 32.04 and 33.01: no demo and no frozen duck.)*

`RAIL: DEC 6, 2023` (draft 8.3: on the cover's delivery)

`[ECU]` [32.02] (3.75 s) DELIVERY. The rack slot ejects a magazine: EMIT's year-end issue. On its cover, full-bleed, Mas, calm, lit like a keynote, under `CEO OF THE YEAR` [V · DEC 6, 2023 · the facts owner confirms it ran as a cover; fallback: the issue falls open to his page].

`[MCU]` [32.03] (≈ 4.8 s; draft 8.2, P19: held a second longer, so the tag breathes once) Mas holds the cover up next to his face. The cover and the face wear the same expression.

**MAS (V.O.)** `[v3-vo-24 · caught · draft 8.1: restored (calibration §5; the audit keeps it): the picture shows the two alike, and only the voice can say which one he thinks is performing]`\
it looks calmer than me.

`[2S]` [32.04] (4 s) The Orb's scan fan sweeps them both. Over Mas the toast reads `verified: human` at once. Over the cover the fan visibly re-sweeps under a toast that reads `re-scanning…`, and the verdict takes two beats longer to arrive: `verified: human`.

`[MCU·PF]` [32.05] (≈ 2.6 s) MAS, looking at the cover, not at the Orb.

**MAS**\
that was close. `[INVENTED · v31-tg-0002 · U8: "close." was ambiguous]`

`[W]` [32.07] (≈ 3.5 s; the dark room's back wall) He pins the cover to the wall. Beside it he hangs a small frame, and inside the frame is the `GUEST` lanyard.

*(v3, C12: the drawer insert, with the CTRL keycap, the check's pen and Terb's pin, is cut. The pin pays off in its own scene: Terb's extinguisher works because the pin is out.)*

### 33. SAME — BUTTON [BASE] · I · P35 sc 58

`SCENE:` A newspaper suing his company and his landlord arrives with a thud. Everything on the desk hops except his water. He says the word he said to Friday's invite. It hooks Ep2 on a sound.

`[W]` [33.01] (2.25 s) SFX: *THUD.* A newspaper drops flat onto the desk from above frame. The felt line stops on the thud; the rack and the fans hold. The room layer shakes 2 px. Everything on the desk hops: the phone, the keyboard, the Orb (one pixel). *(No band prompt, as everywhere in draft 7.)*

`[HIGH]` [33.02] (≈ 4.25 s) THE GREY LADY's masthead, a plain serif (never blackletter), over a front page's columns, so she reads as a newspaper before a word is read. Across the front, the only text under the masthead is a clerk's stamp, held to read: `COMPLAINT · THE GREY LADY v. MACROSOFT & NOPEAI · COPYRIGHT · FILED DEC 27, 2023` [V · DEC 27, 2023 · the facts owner confirms "COPYRIGHT" as the nature of suit]. The page settles twice under the stamp's hold.

`[MCU·PF]` [33.04] (3.5 s) MAS in profile, and in the same frame, at his hand, his glass: everything on the desk hopped, and its water line is flat, not a ripple. *(The glass insert folds in here: one image, one button.)*

**MAS**\
noted. `[INVENTED · the cold open's word]`

MUSIC: the chord with no third, on the downbeat (MM-12's button), held on his face. Under it, heard and not seen, the Q\* vault's F hum carries in and becomes the chord's root.

[33.05] CUT TO BLACK on the hum (1.25 s).

## [OUTRO — the Orb outro (proposal B), written and built by the `v3-outro` pass]

*The Orb turns and scans us (show/production/OUTRO-PROPOSALS.md §B, SHOWRUNNER-NOTES 4). The credits read art, script, music, voices and edit by Opus 5.5, then "prompt: jgon". No parody, terms or disclaimer line. It replaces the 12 s placeholder; it is episode time, not story. Its content is the outro pass's, not this script's.*

## Act Four, draft 5.0 (the conversation pass): what changed

*Draft 4.2 → 5.0, 2026-09-26. Written by the head writer to the showrunner's note on animatic v4 (quoted in the act's header and in [SHOWRUNNER-NOTES](../../production/SHOWRUNNER-NOTES.md) note 11), to the conversation rules R17–R21 in [tone-and-dialogue](../../bible/tone-and-dialogue.md#conversation) and [flow-and-continuity §4](../../bible/flow-and-continuity.md#4-dialogue-rhythm), and from the [dialogue diagnosis](production/act4/dialogue-diagnosis-v4.md) and the [voice diagnosis](production/act4/voice-diagnosis-v4.md). **Nothing in 5.0 has been read aloud, recorded, heard or watched.** Every length is a reading estimate: the words at the voice diagnosis's per-character turn paces (§4.2), plus the picture time each shot describes. The takes set the real frames.*

**What the note measured on v4.** 45 voiced lines and 176 words in 4:11; a median of 4 words a line; 21 lines of three words or fewer; 12 lines cut off or cut into; 21 exchanges averaging 2 lines, the longest 10 s. Speech came in 1–4 s bursts between wordless stretches of about 6.6 s (22.5 s at most), about 480 words of on-screen text outweighed the 176 spoken, and nobody on the board ever spoke to Mas. The causes were the post-v2 tightening, which cut talk instead of dead time; the old R8, "Collide, don't volley", with its overlap-in-every-scene checklist item; and v3.2's retakes, sped up to fit a picture already cut.

**What 5.0 does.**
- **Ten conversations**, each with an ask, a push-back and a turn, in complete thoughts, covered by one held setup with cuts on the turns. They're listed with their lengths in the act's header. The longest are Gerg reading Mas the letter (~51 s), the committee running straight into the call to the rival lab (~62 s) and the terms (~41 s).
- **The counts, estimated from the script:** 95 voiced lines and 683 words (v4: 45 and 176), plus 6 silent posts. The median is 5 words a line overall, and 6 for invented lines by anyone but Mas. Mas stays the shortest speaker in every room (23 lines, median 2 words, every one complete), because the people pressing him now talk in full. 30 lines are three words or fewer; most are Mas's or the kept seasoning ("When?", "Share what?", "More. Soon.", "Good question." ×2, "…Ah.", "hi." / "Hello."). There are three dashes and two designed overlaps in the act (v4: 12 lines cut off or cut into).
- **Somebody talks to Mas.** Alyi on the noon call (in pass one), Gerg at 2 AM, Tasya through the door and again on his floor, Alyi across the gap, Terb and Mada at the table. He answers each of them, briefly and in lowercase, and never states a motive.
- **The newcomer's five gaps** ([v4-for-review §4](production/act4/v4-for-review.md#4-the-must-understand-scorecard)) now arrive inside conversations, each told once:
  1. *Why the board acted, as they see it:* Neleh reads the post aloud, once; Alyi answers "Is this a coup?" with the fuller record ("…the board doing its duty to the mission of the nonprofit…"); Ttemme asks, and the answer is a sealed folder. The why stays a question the show raises on purpose, and Mas asks it himself at the end ("of what?"). Nobody answers.
  2. *Mario's second call:* "Nozama. About the money."
  3. *That the return talks failed, and what happened to Rima:* "He's been downstairs all day, talking about coming back." / "In a guest badge." / "It's the correct badge. He doesn't work here.", then "You already have an interim CEO." / "We'd like a different one."
  4. *The letter's threat:* Gerg reads it to him ("walk where?" / "Macrosoft.").
  5. *What Q\* is:* the rail gets back draft 2's "a breakthrough called", and Gerg's "You said that about the last one." ties it to the launch.
- **The diagnosis's 14 blurts** each get a lead-in, a trigger or a listener:
  - Neleh's blueprint figure is on the sheet from the first word, and Mada's spinner icon turns as he speaks.
  - Rima's tile joins with a chime and answers a question ("For how long?").
  - The employee is already standing; "The bylaws allow it." is folded into an answer to the phones.
  - Alyi's koan gets "When?" / "The company will tell us."; Adelina crosses into the pane and is named; Mario's second call is announced.
  - Ttemme's first words go to the board ("You already have an interim CEO."), so "Chat… for how long?" has a run-up.
  - "mostly." answers the Orb's look at a line Mas says aloud.
  - Gerg's tile rings; Tasya's door turns its key; "below, above, around" answers Mas's question.
  - The memo is read to the staff who stayed.
- **The must-understand list** (v4-for-review §4, 13 beats) is all still carried, and several beats are now both said and shown.
- **The insider's protected list** (v4-for-review §5) is all kept: the tally under "i don't keep score."; "super." twice, bookended by "okay."; Alyi's cursor on Cancel; the step-four runner; "Chat… for how long?" and the hourglass; `VOID IF CEO MISSING`; "leave it open."; the avalanche's exits; "Good question." / "good question." (now answering "of what?" instead of "Terms?"); the memo over the nameplate; the observer chair.
- **Told twice, now with words.** His side of noon is still a dialog box and D6's silence, but Alyi's mouth moves in his tile and no words reach us (draft 2's hinge). Pass one opens a minute earlier, on their side of the same call, and we hear what Alyi said, while Mas's tile doesn't move: "Is his feed frozen?" / "No. That is just him."

**What was cut to make room: empty picture, not talk.**
- *Silent inserts the talk now carries:* the blog post's silent insert (Neleh reads it); the letter's silent page read (Gerg reads over the same page); the term sheet, S7.10 (Terb says the terms); Gerg's return post on its own phone, S7.11 (it rides the two-shot); the lobby's separate box of zeros, S8.02 (folded into S8.01, as v4-for-review §7.5 offered).
- *Plates and a rail the talk now says:* `CEO (WEEKEND EDITION)` on Rima's plate (it answered "For how long?" before she asks); Alyi's plate (the noon tag says `CO-FOUNDER` on both sides); Ttemme's `INTERIM CEO #2 · EX-STREAMING CEO`, now `RAN A STREAMING SITE`; `(REPORTED) OFFERED MAS'S JOB` on Mario's plate; `RAIL: NOV 19 · RETURN TALKS AT HQ` (the lobby camera's own date and Neleh's line).
- *Dead time:* a second of the removed-notice hold (the insider's note); the voiced count of signatures (the counter shows it as Gerg reads); and **Mas's 9:32 post**, "…the nopeai board should go after me for the full value of my shares", with its pen tap (about 4.5 s; the blueprint's `EQUITY: 0` already carries the joke, and the insider marked it long). It can come back in 26A, on his own side, if the room wants his public joke.
- *Lines:* "The bylaws allow it." (folded into "It was. Footnote three.") and "Terms?" (Terb reads the terms). "I've written up some thoughts—" and "One sec. Compiling—" become whole sentences.
- *V.O.:* D3 becomes a line said aloud; the act now has one V.O. line, D2.

**Restored from draft 2** ([dialogue-diagnosis §3](production/act4/dialogue-diagnosis-v4.md#3-draft-2-material-worth-restoring)): Alyi's silent mouth on his side of the call; "For how long?"; "The company will tell us."; Mario's thought finished before Adelina, and her name; the desks through Tasya's door; the new board seated, with THE OTHER YRRAL mute behind his nameplate; "a breakthrough called" on the Q\* rail; the conference-room door with its nameplate still on. **Not restored,** as the diagnosis advised: "Step four." / "Good question." on THE PLAN; Mada's "Good question." ending pass one; `RAIL: +1 FIRING`; the typed `…` boxes. "the hearts were sincere." isn't restored either: 5.0 keeps "the badge was a joke.", now spoken, because the tag's framed lanyard pays off those words.

**Real lines.** Every real line keeps its words and its tag. These are fuller or newly voiced, and each is to re-fetch and log before lock; if one can't be confirmed, the fallback is v4's logged fragment:

| Line (parody names swapped) | Where | Source in the repo |
|---|---|---|
| The blog post's second sentence, "The board no longer has confidence in his ability to continue leading NopeAI." | sc 27, Neleh reads the post | research mid.md §3 (facts #139 logs the first clause) |
| Alyi's all-hands answer: "…I disagree with this. This was the board doing its duty to the mission of the nonprofit…" | sc 27 | mid.md §3; alyi.md (facts #143 logs the first clause). The new tone rules flag it for re-fetching too |
| Tasya: "We look forward to getting to know Ttemme and NopeAI's new leadership team…" | sc 27 | mid.md §3 (the source reads "OAI's") |
| The letter's threat: "…assured us that there are positions for all NopeAI employees…" | sc 29, Gerg reads it | mid.md §3; the exact words (including "that") still to fetch |
| Alyi's regret, second sentence: "I never intended to harm NopeAI…" | sc 30 | mid.md §3 (facts #150 logs the first sentence) |
| Tasya: "…we have all the IP rights and all the capability…" | sc 30 | mid.md §3 ([V/K]) |
| The return announcement: "…an agreement in principle for Mas Manalt to return to NopeAI as CEO with a new initial board of Terb (Chair), [THE OTHER YRRAL], and Mada." | sc 30, Terb reads it | mid.md §3 |
| The memo's middle clause: "i think he's a guiding light of the field and a gem of a human being." | sc 31 | mid.md §3; alyi.md (facts #156 logs the ellipsis form) |

Two items the diagnosis quoted from memory are **not used**: the call's account in Gerg's joint-timeline post (Alyi's noon line follows only the logged facts that Mas was told on the call and that the post followed), and the letter's lines about a replaced interim CEO and "consistent with the mission". The reported Neleh line stays out of her mouth.

**Guardrails and POV.**
- No invented line gives a reason for the firing. The only characterisation of Mas is the post's own wording, and Ttemme reads the reasoning in a sealed folder we never see into (X9).
- Mas never states a motive or an intent at a real event. Gerg's "So are we really going to Macrosoft?" goes unanswered. "leave it open." comes after a full offer, with no look around first (no hover before a choice at a real event, pov-and-framing §3.7): the diagnosis asked to restore draft 2's look, and 5.0 gets the considered answer from the conversation instead. His forgiveness of Alyi is a joke about counting, not a feeling.
- Neleh stays principled, precise and polite, and never answers "not consistently candid". Alyi's regret comes only through his post, and his invented lines are literal and impersonal. Mada never gives an answer, a yes or a no: Terb says "He stays." for him. THE OTHER YRRAL is mute and THE QUIET VOTE never speaks. Tasya never says "no", and there's no accent humour.
- Invented talk stays on procedure, the staff, money and the company. Parody names only.
- **POV.** Pass one is still the one exit, but at about 3:19 it's some 16% of the episode as estimated, past the guide's 12%, because three of the newcomer's gaps happen in the board's rooms. His side grew with it, so the exit is well under half the act. Mas's still tile on their noon call is new; otherwise he appears in pass one only as his posts, the lobby camera and his tinny voice. **A ruling for the showrunner.**
- **Posts read aloud.** R21 lets people read the record to each other. Neleh reads the post, Gerg the letter and Terb the announcement; Tasya and Alyi read their own statements, as v4 already had them do. tone-and-dialogue §5.2 rule 3 still says posts are pop-ups, never speeches, so the tone owner should reconcile the two. Meanwhile the words are exact and tagged.

**Voices.** Of the 95 voiced lines, 52 are new, 12 are changed from v4 (rewritten, extended or moved from V.O. to on-mic), and 31 keep v4's words. Two v4 lines are cut. **All 95 are recorded fresh by the v5 method** (voice-diagnosis §4). The v4 takes were fitted to v4's slots, so even the unchanged words need new reads inside their new scenes. The two interrupted lines (Mario's, Neleh's "char—") are recorded complete.

**Runtime.** Estimated 7:59 (12:31–20:30), +3:48 on 4.2's 4:11.5:

| Chapter | Estimate | v4.2 |
|---|---|---|

- As played, the story would run about 20:23 (from about 16:35 with 4.2). That's inside the pilot's story band (20:45 ±0:30), at its low end.
- It uses nearly all the headroom, about 20 s of it left, and Acts One–Three show the same clipped pattern and will grow when they get their own conversation pass. **The room's ruling on the pilot's length is now the live question.**
- If the act needs to be shorter, these trims keep every must-understand beat and every protected moment. In the order I'd take them, they come to about −40 s (to about 7:20):

**What a human must check** (I can't listen or watch):
